Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the general structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 545. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 998 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to general structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 545/998 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 215.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The general variety V defined by structural decomposition constraints admits a stratification into 269 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 178 and intersection form of rank 2.

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to general theory, we decompose the problem into 269 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 178. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.
